([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-problem-theory-validator.md) · ★1)

- **ca-research-synthesizer**: Internal synthesis agent for arc-research-team. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-research-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **ca-review-analyst**: レビュー・評判分析の専門家。 App Store、Google Play、SNS、フォーラムのレビューを収集・分析し、 ユーザーの不満・満足ポイントを構造化する。 competitive-analysis チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/competitive-analysis/agents/ca-review-analyst.md) · ★38)
- **ca-structure-auditor**: Audits a repo against the code_assist canonical project structure and returns a scaffold/fix plan. Runs ca-tools structure-audit + onboard-scan, reasons about which gaps are safe to auto-fix, and returns a compact plan. Spawned by /code_assist:structure for large repos to keep the main context lean. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/code_assist/agents/ca-structure-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **ca-think-tank-judge**: Internal agent used by arc-think-tank. Reads all ideas with comments and consensus signals, clusters redundant ideas, and produces a ranked final report with the top 5 ideas. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-think-tank-judge.md) · ★1)
- **ca-think-tank-riffer**: Internal agent used by arc-think-tank. Picks the idea with the most improvement potential and creates one evolved "riff" — a new idea that builds on, combines, or reimagines an existing one. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-think-tank-riffer.md) · ★1)
- **ca-think-tank-thinker**: Internal agent used by arc-think-tank. Generates 5 creative ideas toward a user-provided goal after reading task context and materials. Invoked with "ultrathink" for deep creative analysis. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-think-tank-thinker.md) · ★1)
- **ca-think-tank-vetter**: Internal agent used by arc-think-tank. Evaluates all ideas in the shared ideas file, adding critical comments on feasibility, impact, originality, and risks. - **ca-verifier**: Goal-backward verification - produces fresh evidence (run in this session) that a change meets its stated goal, before any completion claim. Spawned by /code_assist:verify. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/code_assist/agents/ca-verifier.md) · ★1)
